# Introduction | FaceFusion

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.facefusion.io/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.facefusion.io/introduction.md)
.

> Industry leading face manipulation platform.

### Preview[](https://docs.facefusion.io/#preview)

![](https://docs.facefusion.io/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2Fcontent.gitbook.com%2Fcontent%2Fb911DLBrQbwvbGi75DSg%2Fblobs%2F3RGxl8RBx4RGc7VxbuAd%2Fscreenshot-3.8.0.png&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=94fbab74&sv=2)

### Installation[](https://docs.facefusion.io/#installation)

Be aware, the [installation](https://docs.facefusion.io/installation)
 needs technical skills and is not recommended for beginners. In case you are not comfortable using a terminal, our [Windows Installer](http://windows-installer.facefusion.io/)
 and [macOS Installer](http://macos-installer.facefusion.io/)
 get you started.

[Installation](https://docs.facefusion.io/installation)

### Usage[](https://docs.facefusion.io/#usage)

Run the command:

Copy

    python facefusion.py [commands] [options]
    
    options:
      -h, --help                                      show this help message and exit
      -v, --version                                   show program's version number and exit
    
    commands:
        run                                           run the program
        headless-run                                  run the program in headless mode
        batch-run                                     run the program in batch mode
        force-download                                force automate downloads and exit
        benchmark                                     benchmark the program
        job-list                                      list jobs by status
        job-create                                    create a drafted job
        job-submit                                    submit a drafted job to become a queued job
        job-submit-all                                submit all drafted jobs to become a queued jobs
        job-delete                                    delete a drafted, queued, failed or completed job
        job-delete-all                                delete all drafted, queued, failed and completed jobs
        job-add-step                                  add a step to a drafted job
        job-remix-step                                remix a previous step from a drafted job
        job-insert-step                               insert a step to a drafted job
        job-remove-step                               remove a step from a drafted job
        job-run                                       run a queued job
        job-run-all                                   run all queued jobs
        job-retry                                     retry a failed job
        job-retry-all                                 retry all failed jobs

# Changelog | 3.7.1 | FaceFusion

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.facefusion.io/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/introduction/changelog.md)
.

### 3.7.1[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/introduction/changelog#id-3.7.1)

*   Adjust frame distribution to restore performance
    
*   Fix image to image workflow when using 2 processors
    

### 3.7.0[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/introduction/changelog#id-3.7.0)

*   Bring in the face tracker to refill faces the face detector misses
    
*   Boost `CoreML` performance for `fp16` models
    
*   Architectural shift to multi frame aware processors
    
*   Reduce overhead via processor driven model loading
    
*   Introduce `auto` mode to the face selector to match source to target
    
*   Improve video compatibility with QuickTime under macOS
    
*   Make the installer take a positional argument rather than `--onnxruntime`
    
*   Remove `--system-memory-limit` argument
    

### 3.6.1[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/introduction/changelog#id-3.6.1)

*   Add `--ssl-no-revoke` to curl since Norton and Avast break certificates
    
*   Fix sanitizer for multiple `int` arguments
    

### 3.6.0[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/introduction/changelog#id-3.6.0)

*   Add `fran` model to the age modifier
    
*   Add `corridor_key_1024` and `corridor_key_2048` models to the background remover
    
*   Revamp conda environment to configure `PATH` and `LD_LIBRARY_PATH` on the startup
    
*   Introduce `--background-remover-despill-color` argument
    
*   Rename `--background-remover-color` to `--background-remover-fill-color`
    
*   Fix incorrect model vendors and licenses
    

### 3.5.4[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/introduction/changelog#id-3.5.4)

*   Introduce experimental support for the `QNN` execution provider
    
*   Prevent invalid execution cache after onnxruntime update
    
*   Hide `ConnectionResetError` by asyncio under Windows
    
*   Secure `--processors`, `--ui-layouts` and `--job-id` to prevent attacks
    
*   Remove `--insecure` from curl to enforce secure connections
    

### 3.5.3[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/introduction/changelog#id-3.5.3)

*   Prevent conflicts with system-wide CUDA under Windows
    
*   Fix extraction of 16-bit video footage like `ProRes`
    
*   Fix webcam stripe artifacts caused by resolution mismatch
    

### 3.5.2[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/introduction/changelog#id-3.5.2)

*   Add retry for downloads to handle weak internet connections
    
*   Introduce optional `--force-reinstall` argument to the installer
    
*   Fix dependency resolution by single run installation
    

### 3.5.1[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/introduction/changelog#id-3.5.1)

*   Fix side by side preview on images with channel mismatch
    
*   Fix inference crash when using `--execution-devices-ids` argument
    

### 3.5.0[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/introduction/changelog#id-3.5.0)

*   Introduce background remover processor
    
*   Add `--face-detector-margin` to detect face beyond the frame
    
*   Add frame processor models `face_dat_x4` and `tghq_face_x8`
    
*   Extend `--output-pattern` for batch-run by source and target values
    
*   Revamp architecture to modular processors and workflows
    
*   Support for the `mpeg` and `mxf` video format
    

### 3.4.2[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/introduction/changelog#id-3.4.2)

*   Fix RAM explosion on 4K footage
    
*   Fix broken bounding box
    

### 3.4.1[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/introduction/changelog#id-3.4.1)

*   Remove `DirectShow` camera backend on Windows
    
*   Fix output processing with frame enhancer
    
*   Fix side by side preview with frame enhancer
    

### 3.4.0[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/introduction/changelog#id-3.4.0)

*   Introduce single pass frame processing
    
*   Process inference on multiple execution devices
    
*   Add side by side preview modes along with preview resolutions
    
*   Introduce `--expression-restorer-areas` argument
    
*   Revamp webcam experience and performance
    
*   Reintroduce the `yunet` face detector
    
*   Add `--voice-extractor-model` argument
    
*   Migrate ArcFace converter to CrossFace models
    
*   Balance source and target using the `--face-swapper-weight` argument
    
*   Introduce `--benchmark-mode` to select between warm and cold caches
    
*   Introduce many for the `--face-occluder-model` argument
    
*   Replace output resolution with relative output scale
    
*   Add `libx264rgb` to avoid color shifts due to RGB to YUV conversion
    
*   Add support for the `wmv` video format
    
*   Experimental support for `MIGraphX` execution provider
    
*   Log model information in debug mode
    
*   Remove `--execution-queue-count` argument
    
*   Remove score and classifier items from the face debugger
    
*   Fix crash on headless-run when passing invalid output path
    
*   Fix demographic filters for mutated faces
    

### 3.3.2[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/introduction/changelog#id-3.3.2)

*   Fix broken Gradio dependency
    
*   Make `hyperswap_1a_256` the default face swapper model
    

### 3.3.1[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/introduction/changelog#id-3.3.1)

*   Fix the face store causing the second processor to fail
    

### 3.3.0[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/introduction/changelog#id-3.3.0)

*   Introduce `hyperswap` models by FaceFusion Labs
    
*   Add more deep swapper models
    
*   Add the `ultra_sharp_2_x4` frame enhancer model
    
*   Boost performance for high resolution processing
    
*   Run multiple NSFW models to prevent false detection
    
*   Use the 68-point face landmarks to create face area masking
    
*   Control lip movements using the `--lip-syncer-weight` argument
    
*   Enhance read and write reliability for corrupted videos
    
*   Introduce fallback logic for unsupported audio and video encoders
    
*   Fix the `UDP` webcam mode
    
*   Fix bitrate range violation for `libopus`
    

### 3.2.0[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/introduction/changelog#id-3.2.0)

*   Introduce `gitee` and `hf-mirror` to bypass China's great firewall
    
*   Enhance NSFW validation using a `YOLO` powered detector
    
*   Reduce choices to installed audio encoders and video encoders
    
*   Introduce `--halt-on-error` for several job commands
    
*   Improve Gradio preview for unplayable videos larger than 512 MB in size
    
*   Make the face selector more responsive to preview changes
    
*   Add `--output-audio-quality` and `--output-audio-volume` arguments
    
*   Use `flac` over `aac` for the `--output-audio-encoder` default
    
*   Extend the face occluder by the `xseg_3` model
    
*   Automatically select the most suitable execution provider
    
*   Enable fast prediction and model caches for CoreML
    
*   Remove `--skip-audio` argument as muted audio yield the same
    
*   Normalize the range of the `--reference-face-distance` argument
    
*   Eliminate broken file format verification in Gradio
    
*   Restore frame colorizer support for CoreML
    
*   Prevent deep swapper and face enhancer errors when offline
    
*   Experimental multi GPU support for OpenVINO
    
*   Fix model size detection of the deep swapper
    
*   Fix inference pool sharing to avoid crash while switching models
    
*   Fix audio restoring when `--output-video-fps` is modified
    
*   Fix progress bar while extracting frames and merging videos
    
*   Fix `webp` and `webm` support under Windows
    

### 3.1.2[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/introduction/changelog#id-3.1.2)

*   Fix broken `pydantic` dependency
    

### 3.1.1[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/introduction/changelog#id-3.1.1)

*   Restore `fp16` fallbacks for CoreML
    
*   Fix CUDA for the Geforce 16 series
    

### 3.1.0[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/introduction/changelog#id-3.1.0)

*   Support for `DeepFaceLive` models
    
*   Fallback aware download providers
    
*   Introduce the `hififace_256` face swapper model
    
*   Polish age modifier processor to reduce halo effect
    
*   Introduce batch processing via the `batch-run` command
    
*   Provide adjustable face occluder and face parser
    
*   Release the popular frame enhancer models
    
*   Add progress bar for extracting and merging of videos
    
*   Modernize the UI to align with the Gradio 5 update
    
*   Provide an option to select the webcam device id
    
*   Introduce `--temp-path` argument to alter temporary resources
    
*   Add video encoder `h264_qsv` and `hevc_qsv`
    
*   Remove obsolete `--skip-download` argument
    
*   Enforce `libvpx-vp9` codec for `webm` processing
    
*   Gracefully close the webcam stream on exit
    
*   Fix inconsistent preview updates
    
*   Fix video length issues when restoring audio
    
*   Fix OpenVINO issue by aliasing `GPU.0` to `GPU`
    

### 3.0.1[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/introduction/changelog#id-3.0.1)

*   Fix broken `python-multipart` dependency
    

### 3.0.0[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/introduction/changelog#id-3.0.0)

*   Revamp the architecture where everything is a job
    
*   Introduce `pixel boost` to the face swappers
    
*   Add multi angle processing to the face detectors
    
*   Introduce age modifier processor
    
*   Introduce Live Portrait powered expression restorer processor
    
*   Introduce Live Portrait powered face editor processor
    
*   Replace face parser with `bisenet_resnet_34` model
    
*   Release the `GHOST` face swapper models
    
*   Release frame enhancer `real_esrgan_x8` models
    
*   Introduce ArcFace converter models for SimSwap and GHOST
    
*   Introduce offline first assets based on local hash validation
    
*   Replace `gender_age` with superior `fairface` model
    
*   Transfer `order`, `age` and `gender` to the face selector
    
*   Support CUDA 12.4, TensorRT 10.4, OpenVINO 2024.1 and ROCm 6.2
    
*   Provide appropriate error codes in headless mode
    
*   Migrate the CLI to commands along with related arguments
    
*   Introduce custom made `peppa_wutz` face landmark model
    
*   Introduce `--face-landmark-model` argument
    
*   Introduce `--output-audio-encoder` argument
    
*   Add video encoder `h264_videotoolbox` and `hevc_videotoolbox`
    
*   Change the `--face-selector-order` default to large-small
    
*   Replace globals with a context aware inference manager
    
*   Use audio whenever defined via source
    
*   Tweak the UI layout and visual appearance
    
*   Update to latest Gradio 4
    
*   Provide a multi range slider to trim video frames
    
*   Introduce a log level aware terminal component to the UI
    
*   Ensure significantly more accurate benchmark results
    
*   Drop the face detector model `yunet` due low accuracy
    
*   Fix flicker in the face swapper `uniface` model
    
*   Fix threading and preview crashes for ROCm and DirectML
    
*   Fix image quality for `webp` images
    
*   Fix the termination of `ffmpeg` processing
    

### 2.6.1[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/introduction/changelog#id-2.6.1)

*   Fix broken `numpy` dependency
    

### 2.6.0[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/introduction/changelog#id-2.6.0)

*   Introduce a standalone one-click installer for Windows
    
*   Add the frame enhancer `clear_reality_x4` and `ultra_sharp_x4` model
    
*   Load different `ini` files via the `--config` argument
    
*   Introduce `--open-browser` to open browser once the program is ready
    
*   Introduce `--execution-device-id` to select processing device
    
*   Support for Intel Arc using the OpenVINO execution provider
    
*   Prevent Gradio from converting videos larger than 512 MB in size
    
*   Fix path issues on Windows related to special characters
    
*   Fix issues when finalizing images using temporary files
    

### 2.5.3[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/introduction/changelog#id-2.5.3)

*   Disable Gradio Analytics to prevent version alerts
    

### 2.5.2[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/introduction/changelog#id-2.5.2)

*   Introduce dynamic frame colorizer sizes
    
*   Validate `facefusion.ini` overrides to prevent crashes
    
*   Remove GPU architecture lookup to support outdated `nvidia-smi`
    
*   Remove `hwaccel` which surprisingly improves the FFmpeg performance
    
*   Fix threading and preview crashes for DirectML
    
*   Fix `--output-video-resolution` from not being applied
    

### 2.5.1[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/introduction/changelog#id-2.5.1)

*   Add missing `deoldify` models to the frame colorizer
    
*   Fix over-correction by voice extractor on background audio
    
*   Fix excessive VRAM consumption of the voice extractor
    
*   Fix exception in `conditional_download` while model removal
    
*   Fix stuck processing when using FFmpeg 7
    

### 2.5.0[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/introduction/changelog#id-2.5.0)

*   Introduce the frame colorizer using the `ddcolor` and `deoldify` model
    
*   Extract voice from the audio to enhance the lip-syncer quality
    
*   Experimental 5 to 68 face landmark estimation
    
*   Add the face enhancer `gpen_bfr_1024` and `gpen_bfr_2048` model
    
*   Add the frame enhancer `real_esrgan_x2` and `real_hatgan_x4` model
    
*   Introduce `--force-download` to download every model at once
    
*   Migrate the documentation and installer from venv to conda
    
*   Full compatibility with CUDA 12 due `inswapper_128_fp16` model fix
    
*   Fix fps boosting and lowering during video merging
    
*   Fix crashes when using `--skip-download` when models are absent
    
*   Fix `h264_nvenc` and `hevc_nvenc` presets mapping
    

### 2.4.1[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/introduction/changelog#id-2.4.1)

*   Conditional download face analyser models
    
*   Prevent download loop when remote is unreachable
    
*   Dynamic `concurrency_count` for ideal Gradio performance
    
*   Monkey patch Gradio for faster preview and stream rendering
    
*   Fix preview for audio to image
    

### 2.4.0[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/introduction/changelog#id-2.4.0)

*   Run multiple face detectors simultaneously
    
*   Prevent the 68 to 5 face landmark transformation on low confidence
    
*   Add the high performance `scrfd` face detector model
    
*   Gracefully start and stop processing
    
*   Introduce ONNX powered frame enhancers
    
*   Introduce `--face-landmarker-score` argument
    
*   Render tabs when using multiple UI layouts
    
*   Introduce `--output-image-resolution` argument
    
*   Add more face debugger items and refine their color theme
    
*   Print internal statistics when `--log-level debug` is enabled
    
*   Keep lips over the whole video once lip syncer is enabled
    
*   Restrict resolution upscaling and fps boosting for temporary resources
    
*   Add support for `h264_amf` and `hevc_amf` video encoders
    
*   Change the `--output-path` behaviour and normalization
    
*   Remove `--temp-frame-quality` argument
    
*   Fix green line when using a target with uneven pixels
    
*   Fix preview of lip syncer when using `--trim-frame-start`
    
*   Fix the blank output issue for Geforce GTX 16 series
    

### 2.3.0[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/introduction/changelog#id-2.3.0)

*   Introduce the lip-syncer processors using `wave2lip`
    
*   Improve face alignment with 68 to 5 face landmark transformation
    
*   Add face swapper model `uniface_256`
    
*   Add `yoloface` as the default face detector model
    
*   Clear temporary resources before processing
    
*   Add `age` and `gender` to the face debugger items
    
*   Update installer to version specific CUDA and ROCM entries
    
*   Improve the description of the CLI arguments
    
*   Enforce `--output-video-resolution` after upscaling
    
*   Reduce terminal output noise while processing
    

### 2.2.1[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/introduction/changelog#id-2.2.1)

*   Replace `restoreformer` with the latest `restoreformer_plus_plus` model
    
*   Fix error when the `facefusion.ini` file is missing a section
    
*   Fix image to image when utilizing `--video-memory-strategy tolerant`
    

### 2.2.0[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/introduction/changelog#id-2.2.0)

*   Significant improve the CUDA performance
    
*   Allow default overrides through the `facefusion.ini` file
    
*   Enable faster `inswapper_128_fp16` model as default
    
*   Add `--video-memory-strategy` to trade VRAM usage for speed
    
*   Add `bmp` to the `--temp-frame-format` argument
    
*   Add compatibility with Pinokio 1.0.0
    
*   Display the processing time in the terminal on success
    
*   Introduce `--output-video-preset` which defaults to `veryfast`
    
*   Introduce `--output-video-fps` argument
    
*   Introduce `--output-video-resolution` argument
    
*   Block the preview and webcam until models are fully downloaded
    
*   Rename `--max-memory` to `--system-memory-limit`
    
*   Fix gender detection due incorrect matrix transformation
    
*   Fix render issues when toggling face debugger items repeatedly
    

### 2.1.3[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/introduction/changelog#id-2.1.3)

*   Fix installer option for `directml` under Windows
    

### 2.1.2[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/introduction/changelog#id-2.1.2)

*   Add CUDA nightly to the installer
    
*   Refresh reference faces on processor change
    
*   Fix rounding issues of number based CLI arguments
    

### 2.1.1[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/introduction/changelog#id-2.1.1)

*   Remove falsy clear reference faces in preview
    

### 2.1.0[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/introduction/changelog#id-2.1.0)

*   Add face occlusion masking
    
*   Add face region masking with selectable regions
    
*   Average source face from multiple images
    
*   Multi processing aware reference faces
    
*   Only process the reference with face enhancer
    
*   Introduce `debug`, `info`, `warn` and `error` log levels
    
*   Restore face alignment offset from retinaface
    
*   Enforce virtual environment via installer
    
*   Reduce the installer options according to platform
    
*   Add `onnxruntime-rocm` support to installer
    
*   Fix preview updates on slider release
    
*   Fix preview updates on face reference change
    
*   Fix similarity transformation for the face alignment
    

### 2.0.0[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/introduction/changelog#id-2.0.0)

*   Add `blendswap_256`, `simswap_256` and `simswap_512_unofficial` to the face swapper
    
*   Add `gpen_bfr_256` and `restoreformer` to the face enhancer
    
*   High performance face detector using the `retinaface` or `yunet` models
    
*   Add ROCm and OpenVINO support to the frame enhancers
    
*   Add support for `webp` images and `webm` videos
    
*   Add the `best-worst` and `worst-best` to `--face-analyser-order`
    
*   Introduce the `face_debugger` to visualize the bounding box, key points and face mask
    
*   Introduce the `facefusion-pinokio` repository to our GitHub organization
    
*   Introduce `--face-mask-blur` and `--face-mask-padding` arguments
    
*   Introduce `--face-detector-size` and `--face-detector-score` arguments
    
*   Extend face selector by `one` mode
    
*   Remove `insightface` dependency due handcrafted frame processing
    
*   Remove `tensorflow` and `PIL` dependencies duo handcrafted frame analysing
    
*   Simplify progress bar to reduce performance hits
    
*   Improve reference face UI for large amount of people
    
*   Webcam mode `udp` and `v4l2` now with solid 25fps at 1080p
    
*   Seamless start and stop the webcam feed
    
*   Rename `--face-recognition` to `--face-selector-mode`
    
*   Rename `--face-analyser-direction` to `--face-analyser-order`
    
*   Fix output validation after processing
    
*   Apply fixed ranges to number based CLI arguments
    
*   Normalize the `--reference-face-distance` calculation to a 0-1 range
    
*   Normalize the `--output-path` even without specified `--source-path`
    
*   Set a 10 seconds timeout for download lookups
    

### 1.3.1[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/introduction/changelog#id-1.3.1)

*   Refine `default` and `cpu` choices for the installer
    

### 1.3.0[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/introduction/changelog#id-1.3.0)

*   Add high performance `fp16` version to the face swapper
    
*   Add ONNX powered `CodeFormer`, `GFPGAN` and `GPEN` to the face enhancer
    
*   Add `RealESRGAN_x2plus` and `RealESRNet_x4plus` to the frame enhancer
    
*   Implement blending for the face enhancer and frame enhancer
    
*   Enable processors to have options and CLI arguments
    
*   Refine the UI theme to a minimal and flat design
    
*   Enhance the `--help` and documentation with arguments grouping
    
*   Reduce the default image and video quality to 80 percent
    
*   Introduce a new `--torch` argument to the installer
    
*   Improve Apple Silicon performance using the latest `onnxruntime`
    
*   Fix video quality to compression normalization
    
*   Fix stuck downloads caused by the Windows firewall
    

### 1.2.1[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/introduction/changelog#id-1.2.1)

*   Perform VRAM clearance after each processing step
    
*   Restrict TensorFlow memory consumption to 512 MB
    
*   Verify the model files before frame processing
    
*   Introduce `--skip-download` to skip automated downloads
    
*   Upgrade to the most recent `onnxruntime` version
    

### 1.2.0[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/introduction/changelog#id-1.2.0)

*   Establish realtime webcam performance
    
*   Optimize reruns by caching the face analyser results
    
*   Optimize image read performance through caching
    
*   Resume interrupted asset and model downloads
    
*   Allow passing `--onnxruntime` to `install.py`
    
*   Introduce official `facefusion-colab` repository
    
*   Remove alignments from each UI components
    
*   Introduce checkbox group for the settings
    
*   Set webcam resolution and fps in the UI
    
*   Disallow empty execution providers in the UI
    
*   Fix frame range processing on videos with audio
    
*   Fix `CUDA` and `ROCM` Dockerfile to utilize the GPU
    
*   Fix sorting of temp frame paths
    

### 1.1.0[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/introduction/changelog#id-1.1.0)

*   Installer for common and acceleration dependencies
    
*   Built-in webcam suite that supports `udp` and `v4l2` streaming
    
*   Dedicated `--headless` mode
    
*   Add Docker support for ROCM
    
*   Allow adjustment of `--output-path` via the UI
    
*   Enhance output path normalization
    
*   Single warm-up before starting the benchmark suite
    
*   Ability to select runs for the benchmark suite
    
*   Enable benchmarking with partial updates
    
*   Implement a fully event-driven UI for super responsiveness
    
*   Add a max memory slider to select free RAM
    
*   Implement conditional UI based on target media type
    
*   Introduce `--output-image-quality` option for image output
    
*   Fix `crf` range for `libvpx` video encoder
    
*   Fix `detect_fps` to work with different encoding
    

### 1.0.0[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/introduction/changelog#id-1.0.0)

*   Dynamic UI based on Gradio
    
*   Docker support for CPU and CUDA
    
*   Replace `--many-faces` by `--face-recognition` that takes `reference` and `many` value
    
*   Twin support: swap multiple faces by one face reference
    
*   Introduce `--execution-queue-count` to adjust processing per thread
    
*   Rename `--execution-threads` to `--execution-thread-count`
    
*   Global options direction, age and gender for face analyser
    
*   Blur preview and processing hold when NSFW content has been detected
    
*   Flip the quality best to worse from 0-100 to 100-0
    
*   Trim video via `trim-frame-start` and `trim-frame-end`
    
*   Built-in benchmark suite
    
*   Intensive codebase refactoring
    

Last updated 1 month ago

We acknowledge the unethical potential of FaceFusion and are resolutely dedicated to establishing safeguards against such misuse. This software has been engineered to abstain from processing inappropriate content such as nudity, graphic content and sensitive material.

It is important to note that we maintain a strong stance against any type of pornographic nature and do not collaborate with any websites promoting the unauthorized use of our software.

Users who seek to engage in such activities will face consequences, including being banned from our community or reported to public authorities in case of legal violations.

Markdown versions of documentation pages are available by appending \`.md\` to page URLs; this page is available as \[Markdown\](https://docs.facefusion.io/introduction.md). # Introduction > Industry leading face manipulation platform. ### Preview

![](https://docs.facefusion.io/files/4N1XcOqFeA7RnZ4orFC2)

\### Installation Be aware, the \[installation\](https://docs.facefusion.io/installation) needs technical skills and is not recommended for beginners. In case you are not comfortable using a terminal, our \[Windows Installer\](http://windows-installer.facefusion.io) and \[macOS Installer\](http://macos-installer.facefusion.io) get you started. {% content-ref url="/pages/8FlJQJUFfhREv2LUOc1O" %} \[Installation\](/installation.md) {% endcontent-ref %} ### Usage Run the command: \`\`\` python facefusion.py \[commands\] \[options\] options: -h, --help show this help message and exit -v, --version show program's version number and exit commands: run run the program headless-run run the program in headless mode batch-run run the program in batch mode force-download force automate downloads and exit benchmark benchmark the program job-list list jobs by status job-create create a drafted job job-submit submit a drafted job to become a queued job job-submit-all submit all drafted jobs to become a queued jobs job-delete delete a drafted, queued, failed or completed job job-delete-all delete all drafted, queued, failed and completed jobs job-add-step add a step to a drafted job job-remix-step remix a previous step from a drafted job job-insert-step insert a step to a drafted job job-remove-step remove a step from a drafted job job-run run a queued job job-run-all run all queued jobs job-retry retry a failed job job-retry-all retry all failed jobs \`\`\`

Changelog ### 3.7.1 \* Adjust frame distribution to restore performance \* Fix image to image workflow when using 2 processors ### 3.7.0 \* Bring in the face tracker to refill faces the face detector misses \* Boost \`CoreML\` performance for \`fp16\` models \* Architectural shift to multi frame aware processors \* Reduce overhead via processor driven model loading \* Introduce \`auto\` mode to the face selector to match source to target \* Improve video compatibility with QuickTime under macOS \* Make the installer take a positional argument rather than \`--onnxruntime\` \* Remove \`--system-memory-limit\` argument ### 3.6.1 \* Add \`--ssl-no-revoke\` to curl since Norton and Avast break certificates \* Fix sanitizer for multiple \`int\` arguments ### 3.6.0 \* Add \`fran\` model to the age modifier \* Add \`corridor\_key\_1024\` and \`corridor\_key\_2048\` models to the background remover \* Revamp conda environment to configure \`PATH\` and \`LD\_LIBRARY\_PATH\` on the startup \* Introduce \`--background-remover-despill-color\` argument \* Rename \`--background-remover-color\` to \`--background-remover-fill-color\` \* Fix incorrect model vendors and licenses ### 3.5.4 \* Introduce experimental support for the \`QNN\` execution provider \* Prevent invalid execution cache after onnxruntime update \* Hide \`ConnectionResetError\` by asyncio under Windows \* Secure \`--processors\`, \`--ui-layouts\` and \`--job-id\` to prevent attacks \* Remove \`--insecure\` from curl to enforce secure connections ### 3.5.3 \* Prevent conflicts with system-wide CUDA under Windows \* Fix extraction of 16-bit video footage like \`ProRes\` \* Fix webcam stripe artifacts caused by resolution mismatch ### 3.5.2 \* Add retry for downloads to handle weak internet connections \* Introduce optional \`--force-reinstall\` argument to the installer \* Fix dependency resolution by single run installation ### 3.5.1 \* Fix side by side preview on images with channel mismatch \* Fix inference crash when using \`--execution-devices-ids\` argument ### 3.5.0 \* Introduce background remover processor \* Add \`--face-detector-margin\` to detect face beyond the frame \* Add frame processor models \`face\_dat\_x4\` and \`tghq\_face\_x8\` \* Extend \`--output-pattern\` for batch-run by source and target values \* Revamp architecture to modular processors and workflows \* Support for the \`mpeg\` and \`mxf\` video format ### 3.4.2 \* Fix RAM explosion on 4K footage \* Fix broken bounding box ### 3.4.1 \* Remove \`DirectShow\` camera backend on Windows \* Fix output processing with frame enhancer \* Fix side by side preview with frame enhancer ### 3.4.0 \* Introduce single pass frame processing \* Process inference on multiple execution devices \* Add side by side preview modes along with preview resolutions \* Introduce \`--expression-restorer-areas\` argument \* Revamp webcam experience and performance \* Reintroduce the \`yunet\` face detector \* Add \`--voice-extractor-model\` argument \* Migrate ArcFace converter to CrossFace models \* Balance source and target using the \`--face-swapper-weight\` argument \* Introduce \`--benchmark-mode\` to select between warm and cold caches \* Introduce many for the \`--face-occluder-model\` argument \* Replace output resolution with relative output scale \* Add \`libx264rgb\` to avoid color shifts due to RGB to YUV conversion \* Add support for the \`wmv\` video format \* Experimental support for \`MIGraphX\` execution provider \* Log model information in debug mode \* Remove \`--execution-queue-count\` argument \* Remove score and classifier items from the face debugger \* Fix crash on headless-run when passing invalid output path \* Fix demographic filters for mutated faces ### 3.3.2 \* Fix broken Gradio dependency \* Make \`hyperswap\_1a\_256\` the default face swapper model ### 3.3.1 \* Fix the face store causing the second processor to fail ### 3.3.0 \* Introduce \`hyperswap\` models by FaceFusion Labs \* Add more deep swapper models \* Add the \`ultra\_sharp\_2\_x4\` frame enhancer model \* Boost performance for high resolution processing \* Run multiple NSFW models to prevent false detection \* Use the 68-point face landmarks to create face area masking \* Control lip movements using the \`--lip-syncer-weight\` argument \* Enhance read and write reliability for corrupted videos \* Introduce fallback logic for unsupported audio and video encoders \* Fix the \`UDP\` webcam mode \* Fix bitrate range violation for \`libopus\` ### 3.2.0 \* Introduce \`gitee\` and \`hf-mirror\` to bypass China's great firewall \* Enhance NSFW validation using a \`YOLO\` powered detector \* Reduce choices to installed audio encoders and video encoders \* Introduce \`--halt-on-error\` for several job commands \* Improve Gradio preview for unplayable videos larger than 512 MB in size \* Make the face selector more responsive to preview changes \* Add \`--output-audio-quality\` and \`--output-audio-volume\` arguments \* Use \`flac\` over \`aac\` for the \`--output-audio-encoder\` default \* Extend the face occluder by the \`xseg\_3\` model \* Automatically select the most suitable execution provider \* Enable fast prediction and model caches for CoreML \* Remove \`--skip-audio\` argument as muted audio yield the same \* Normalize the range of the \`--reference-face-distance\` argument \* Eliminate broken file format verification in Gradio \* Restore frame colorizer support for CoreML \* Prevent deep swapper and face enhancer errors when offline \* Experimental multi GPU support for OpenVINO \* Fix model size detection of the deep swapper \* Fix inference pool sharing to avoid crash while switching models \* Fix audio restoring when \`--output-video-fps\` is modified \* Fix progress bar while extracting frames and merging videos \* Fix \`webp\` and \`webm\` support under Windows ### 3.1.2 \* Fix broken \`pydantic\` dependency ### 3.1.1 \* Restore \`fp16\` fallbacks for CoreML \* Fix CUDA for the Geforce 16 series ### 3.1.0 \* Support for \`DeepFaceLive\` models \* Fallback aware download providers \* Introduce the \`hififace\_256\` face swapper model \* Polish age modifier processor to reduce halo effect \* Introduce batch processing via the \`batch-run\` command \* Provide adjustable face occluder and face parser \* Release the popular frame enhancer models \* Add progress bar for extracting and merging of videos \* Modernize the UI to align with the Gradio 5 update \* Provide an option to select the webcam device id \* Introduce \`--temp-path\` argument to alter temporary resources \* Add video encoder \`h264\_qsv\` and \`hevc\_qsv\` \* Remove obsolete \`--skip-download\` argument \* Enforce \`libvpx-vp9\` codec for \`webm\` processing \* Gracefully close the webcam stream on exit \* Fix inconsistent preview updates \* Fix video length issues when restoring audio \* Fix OpenVINO issue by aliasing \`GPU.0\` to \`GPU\` ### 3.0.1 \* Fix broken \`python-multipart\` dependency ### 3.0.0 \* Revamp the architecture where everything is a job \* Introduce \`pixel boost\` to the face swappers \* Add multi angle processing to the face detectors \* Introduce age modifier processor \* Introduce Live Portrait powered expression restorer processor \* Introduce Live Portrait powered face editor processor \* Replace face parser with \`bisenet\_resnet\_34\` model \* Release the \`GHOST\` face swapper models \* Release frame enhancer \`real\_esrgan\_x8\` models \* Introduce ArcFace converter models for SimSwap and GHOST \* Introduce offline first assets based on local hash validation \* Replace \`gender\_age\` with superior \`fairface\` model \* Transfer \`order\`, \`age\` and \`gender\` to the face selector \* Support CUDA 12.4, TensorRT 10.4, OpenVINO 2024.1 and ROCm 6.2 \* Provide appropriate error codes in headless mode \* Migrate the CLI to commands along with related arguments \* Introduce custom made \`peppa\_wutz\` face landmark model \* Introduce \`--face-landmark-model\` argument \* Introduce \`--output-audio-encoder\` argument \* Add video encoder \`h264\_videotoolbox\` and \`hevc\_videotoolbox\` \* Change the \`--face-selector-order\` default to large-small \* Replace globals with a context aware inference manager \* Use audio whenever defined via source \* Tweak the UI layout and visual appearance \* Update to latest Gradio 4 \* Provide a multi range slider to trim video frames \* Introduce a log level aware terminal component to the UI \* Ensure significantly more accurate benchmark results \* Drop the face detector model \`yunet\` due low accuracy \* Fix flicker in the face swapper \`uniface\` model \* Fix threading and preview crashes for ROCm and DirectML \* Fix image quality for \`webp\` images \* Fix the termination of \`ffmpeg\` processing ### 2.6.1 \* Fix broken \`numpy\` dependency ### 2.6.0 \* Introduce a standalone one-click installer for Windows \* Add the frame enhancer \`clear\_reality\_x4\` and \`ultra\_sharp\_x4\` model \* Load different \`ini\` files via the \`--config\` argument \* Introduce \`--open-browser\` to open browser once the program is ready \* Introduce \`--execution-device-id\` to select processing device \* Support for Intel Arc using the OpenVINO execution provider \* Prevent Gradio from converting videos larger than 512 MB in size \* Fix path issues on Windows related to special characters \* Fix issues when finalizing images using temporary files ### 2.5.3 \* Disable Gradio Analytics to prevent version alerts ### 2.5.2 \* Introduce dynamic frame colorizer sizes \* Validate \`facefusion.ini\` overrides to prevent crashes \* Remove GPU architecture lookup to support outdated \`nvidia-smi\` \* Remove \`hwaccel\` which surprisingly improves the FFmpeg performance \* Fix threading and preview crashes for DirectML \* Fix \`--output-video-resolution\` from not being applied ### 2.5.1 \* Add missing \`deoldify\` models to the frame colorizer \* Fix over-correction by voice extractor on background audio \* Fix excessive VRAM consumption of the voice extractor \* Fix exception in \`conditional\_download\` while model removal \* Fix stuck processing when using FFmpeg 7 ### 2.5.0 \* Introduce the frame colorizer using the \`ddcolor\` and \`deoldify\` model \* Extract voice from the audio to enhance the lip-syncer quality \* Experimental 5 to 68 face landmark estimation \* Add the face enhancer \`gpen\_bfr\_1024\` and \`gpen\_bfr\_2048\` model \* Add the frame enhancer \`real\_esrgan\_x2\` and \`real\_hatgan\_x4\` model \* Introduce \`--force-download\` to download every model at once \* Migrate the documentation and installer from venv to conda \* Full compatibility with CUDA 12 due \`inswapper\_128\_fp16\` model fix \* Fix fps boosting and lowering during video merging \* Fix crashes when using \`--skip-download\` when models are absent \* Fix \`h264\_nvenc\` and \`hevc\_nvenc\` presets mapping ### 2.4.1 \* Conditional download face analyser models \* Prevent download loop when remote is unreachable \* Dynamic \`concurrency\_count\` for ideal Gradio performance \* Monkey patch Gradio for faster preview and stream rendering \* Fix preview for audio to image ### 2.4.0 \* Run multiple face detectors simultaneously \* Prevent the 68 to 5 face landmark transformation on low confidence \* Add the high performance \`scrfd\` face detector model \* Gracefully start and stop processing \* Introduce ONNX powered frame enhancers \* Introduce \`--face-landmarker-score\` argument \* Render tabs when using multiple UI layouts \* Introduce \`--output-image-resolution\` argument \* Add more face debugger items and refine their color theme \* Print internal statistics when \`--log-level debug\` is enabled \* Keep lips over the whole video once lip syncer is enabled \* Restrict resolution upscaling and fps boosting for temporary resources \* Add support for \`h264\_amf\` and \`hevc\_amf\` video encoders \* Change the \`--output-path\` behaviour and normalization \* Remove \`--temp-frame-quality\` argument \* Fix green line when using a target with uneven pixels \* Fix preview of lip syncer when using \`--trim-frame-start\` \* Fix the blank output issue for Geforce GTX 16 series ### 2.3.0 \* Introduce the lip-syncer processors using \`wave2lip\` \* Improve face alignment with 68 to 5 face landmark transformation \* Add face swapper model \`uniface\_256\` \* Add \`yoloface\` as the default face detector model \* Clear temporary resources before processing \* Add \`age\` and \`gender\` to the face debugger items \* Update installer to version specific CUDA and ROCM entries \* Improve the description of the CLI arguments \* Enforce \`--output-video-resolution\` after upscaling \* Reduce terminal output noise while processing ### 2.2.1 \* Replace \`restoreformer\` with the latest \`restoreformer\_plus\_plus\` model \* Fix error when the \`facefusion.ini\` file is missing a section \* Fix image to image when utilizing \`--video-memory-strategy tolerant\` ### 2.2.0 \* Significant improve the CUDA performance \* Allow default overrides through the \`facefusion.ini\` file \* Enable faster \`inswapper\_128\_fp16\` model as default \* Add \`--video-memory-strategy\` to trade VRAM usage for speed \* Add \`bmp\` to the \`--temp-frame-format\` argument \* Add compatibility with Pinokio 1.0.0 \* Display the processing time in the terminal on success \* Introduce \`--output-video-preset\` which defaults to \`veryfast\` \* Introduce \`--output-video-fps\` argument \* Introduce \`--output-video-resolution\` argument \* Block the preview and webcam until models are fully downloaded \* Rename \`--max-memory\` to \`--system-memory-limit\` \* Fix gender detection due incorrect matrix transformation \* Fix render issues when toggling face debugger items repeatedly ### 2.1.3 \* Fix installer option for \`directml\` under Windows ### 2.1.2 \* Add CUDA nightly to the installer \* Refresh reference faces on processor change \* Fix rounding issues of number based CLI arguments ### 2.1.1 \* Remove falsy clear reference faces in preview ### 2.1.0 \* Add face occlusion masking \* Add face region masking with selectable regions \* Average source face from multiple images \* Multi processing aware reference faces \* Only process the reference with face enhancer \* Introduce \`debug\`, \`info\`, \`warn\` and \`error\` log levels \* Restore face alignment offset from retinaface \* Enforce virtual environment via installer \* Reduce the installer options according to platform \* Add \`onnxruntime-rocm\` support to installer \* Fix preview updates on slider release \* Fix preview updates on face reference change \* Fix similarity transformation for the face alignment ### 2.0.0 \* Add \`blendswap\_256\`, \`simswap\_256\` and \`simswap\_512\_unofficial\` to the face swapper \* Add \`gpen\_bfr\_256\` and \`restoreformer\` to the face enhancer \* High performance face detector using the \`retinaface\` or \`yunet\` models \* Add ROCm and OpenVINO support to the frame enhancers \* Add support for \`webp\` images and \`webm\` videos \* Add the \`best-worst\` and \`worst-best\` to \`--face-analyser-order\` \* Introduce the \`face\_debugger\` to visualize the bounding box, key points and face mask \* Introduce the \`facefusion-pinokio\` repository to our GitHub organization \* Introduce \`--face-mask-blur\` and \`--face-mask-padding\` arguments \* Introduce \`--face-detector-size\` and \`--face-detector-score\` arguments \* Extend face selector by \`one\` mode \* Remove \`insightface\` dependency due handcrafted frame processing \* Remove \`tensorflow\` and \`PIL\` dependencies duo handcrafted frame analysing \* Simplify progress bar to reduce performance hits \* Improve reference face UI for large amount of people \* Webcam mode \`udp\` and \`v4l2\` now with solid 25fps at 1080p \* Seamless start and stop the webcam feed \* Rename \`--face-recognition\` to \`--face-selector-mode\` \* Rename \`--face-analyser-direction\` to \`--face-analyser-order\` \* Fix output validation after processing \* Apply fixed ranges to number based CLI arguments \* Normalize the \`--reference-face-distance\` calculation to a 0-1 range \* Normalize the \`--output-path\` even without specified \`--source-path\` \* Set a 10 seconds timeout for download lookups ### 1.3.1 \* Refine \`default\` and \`cpu\` choices for the installer ### 1.3.0 \* Add high performance \`fp16\` version to the face swapper \* Add ONNX powered \`CodeFormer\`, \`GFPGAN\` and \`GPEN\` to the face enhancer \* Add \`RealESRGAN\_x2plus\` and \`RealESRNet\_x4plus\` to the frame enhancer \* Implement blending for the face enhancer and frame enhancer \* Enable processors to have options and CLI arguments \* Refine the UI theme to a minimal and flat design \* Enhance the \`--help\` and documentation with arguments grouping \* Reduce the default image and video quality to 80 percent \* Introduce a new \`--torch\` argument to the installer \* Improve Apple Silicon performance using the latest \`onnxruntime\` \* Fix video quality to compression normalization \* Fix stuck downloads caused by the Windows firewall ### 1.2.1 \* Perform VRAM clearance after each processing step \* Restrict TensorFlow memory consumption to 512 MB \* Verify the model files before frame processing \* Introduce \`--skip-download\` to skip automated downloads \* Upgrade to the most recent \`onnxruntime\` version ### 1.2.0 \* Establish realtime webcam performance \* Optimize reruns by caching the face analyser results \* Optimize image read performance through caching \* Resume interrupted asset and model downloads \* Allow passing \`--onnxruntime\` to \`install.py\` \* Introduce official \`facefusion-colab\` repository \* Remove alignments from each UI components \* Introduce checkbox group for the settings \* Set webcam resolution and fps in the UI \* Disallow empty execution providers in the UI \* Fix frame range processing on videos with audio \* Fix \`CUDA\` and \`ROCM\` Dockerfile to utilize the GPU \* Fix sorting of temp frame paths ### 1.1.0 \* Installer for common and acceleration dependencies \* Built-in webcam suite that supports \`udp\` and \`v4l2\` streaming \* Dedicated \`--headless\` mode \* Add Docker support for ROCM \* Allow adjustment of \`--output-path\` via the UI \* Enhance output path normalization \* Single warm-up before starting the benchmark suite \* Ability to select runs for the benchmark suite \* Enable benchmarking with partial updates \* Implement a fully event-driven UI for super responsiveness \* Add a max memory slider to select free RAM \* Implement conditional UI based on target media type \* Introduce \`--output-image-quality\` option for image output \* Fix \`crf\` range for \`libvpx\` video encoder \* Fix \`detect\_fps\` to work with different encoding ### 1.0.0 \* Dynamic UI based on Gradio \* Docker support for CPU and CUDA \* Replace \`--many-faces\` by \`--face-recognition\` that takes \`reference\` and \`many\` value \* Twin support: swap multiple faces by one face reference \* Introduce \`--execution-queue-count\` to adjust processing per thread \* Rename \`--execution-threads\` to \`--execution-thread-count\` \* Global options direction, age and gender for face analyser \* Blur preview and processing hold when NSFW content has been detected \* Flip the quality best to worse from 0-100 to 100-0 \* Trim video via \`trim-frame-start\` and \`trim-frame-end\` \* Built-in benchmark suite \* Intensive codebase refactoring

Copy

    apt install git-all

Copy

    apt install curl

### Conda[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/installation/platform/linux#conda)

Copy

    bash Miniconda3-latest-Linux-x86_64.sh

    apt install ffmpeg

Activate the environment:

Finally, run the program:

\> For the complete documentation index, see \[llms.txt\](https://docs.facefusion.io/llms.txt). Markdown versions of documentation pages are available by appending \`.md\` to page URLs; this page is available as \[Markdown\](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/installation.md).

# Installation

### 1. Prepare Your Platform

{% content-ref url="/pages/ptDjEJiQYmfMKwnxmnC4" %}
\[Linux\](/3.7.1/installation/platform/linux.md)
{% endcontent-ref %}

{% content-ref url="/pages/f5VPIJhtmtQI6rHI2jW6" %}
\[macOS\](/3.7.1/installation/platform/macos.md)
{% endcontent-ref %}

{% content-ref url="/pages/mM490Q5CntMz0QwbTjBC" %}
\[Windows\](/3.7.1/installation/platform/windows.md)
{% endcontent-ref %}

### 2. Prepare Your Environment

Initialize conda for your terminal:

\`\`\`
conda init --all
\`\`\`

Create the environment:

\`\`\`
conda create --name facefusion python=3.12 pip=25.0
\`\`\`

Activate the environment:

\`\`\`
conda activate facefusion
\`\`\`

### 3. Install Your Accelerator

{% content-ref url="/pages/WSUlX58OkGceZcsC8ImV" %}
\[Linux\](/3.7.1/installation/accelerator/linux.md)
{% endcontent-ref %}

{% content-ref url="/pages/0nysjox1RHxmvx1S1abS" %}
\[Windows\](/3.7.1/installation/accelerator/windows.md)
{% endcontent-ref %}

### 4. Download Your Copy

Clone the repository:

\`\`\`
git clone https://github.com/facefusion/facefusion
\`\`\`

Ensure to enter the directory:

\`\`\`
cd facefusion
\`\`\`

### 5. Install The Application

{% tabs fullWidth="false" %}
{% tab title="CPU" %}

\`\`\`
python install.py default
\`\`\`

{% endtab %}

{% tab title="CUDA" %}

\`\`\`
python install.py cuda
\`\`\`

{% endtab %}

{% tab title="CoreML" %}

\`\`\`
python install.py default
\`\`\`

{% endtab %}

{% tab title="DirectML" %}

\`\`\`
python install.py directml
\`\`\`

{% endtab %}

{% tab title="MIGraphX" %}

\`\`\`
python install.py migraphx
\`\`\`

{% endtab %}

{% tab title="OpenVINO" %}

\`\`\`
python install.py openvino
\`\`\`

{% endtab %}
{% endtabs %}

### 6. Reload Your Environment

\`\`\`
conda deactivate
\`\`\`

\`\`\`
conda activate facefusion
\`\`\`

### 7. Done

Finally, run the program:

\`\`\`
python facefusion.py run --open-browser
\`\`\`

# Windows | 3.7.1 | FaceFusion

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.facefusion.io/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/installation/accelerator/windows.md)
.

### CUDA[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/installation/accelerator/windows#cuda)

Compatible with NVIDIA graphic cards:

Copy

    conda install nvidia/label/cuda-12.9.1::cuda-runtime nvidia/label/cudnn-9.10.0::cudnn

### TensorRT[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/installation/accelerator/windows#tensorrt)

Suitable for high performance NVIDIA graphic cards:

Copy

    pip install tensorrt==10.12.0.36 --extra-index-url https://pypi.nvidia.com

### OpenVINO[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/installation/accelerator/windows#openvino)

Suitable for Intel Arc graphic cards:

Copy

    conda install conda-forge::openvino=2025.3.0

*   [CUDA](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/installation/accelerator/windows#cuda)
    
*   [TensorRT](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/installation/accelerator/windows#tensorrt)

# Linux | 3.7.1 | FaceFusion

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.facefusion.io/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/installation/accelerator/linux.md)
.

### CUDA[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/installation/accelerator/linux#cuda)

Compatible with NVIDIA graphic cards:

Copy

    conda install nvidia/label/cuda-12.9.1::cuda-runtime nvidia/label/cudnn-9.10.0::cudnn

### TensorRT[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/installation/accelerator/linux#tensorrt)

Suitable for high performance NVIDIA graphic cards:

Copy

    pip install tensorrt==10.12.0.36 --extra-index-url https://pypi.nvidia.com

### ROCm[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/installation/accelerator/linux#rocm)

Compatible with AMD graphic cards:

Copy

    curl --output amdgpu-install-7.2.deb https://repo.radeon.com/amdgpu-install/7.2/ubuntu/jammy/amdgpu-install_7.2.70200-1_all.deb

Copy

    dpkg --install amdgpu-install-7.2.deb

Copy

    conda install conda-forge::gcc=15.2.0

### OpenVINO[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/installation/accelerator/linux#openvino)

Compatible with Intel graphic cards:

Copy

    conda install conda-forge::openvino=2025.3.0

# FAQ | 3.7.1 | FaceFusion

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.facefusion.io/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/faq.md)
.

#### Are there plans to implement a high resolution inswapper?[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/faq#are-there-plans-to-implement-a-high-resolution-inswapper)

InsightFace opted against releasing those models due ethical considerations.

#### How to improve the quality of swapped faces?[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/faq#how-to-improve-the-quality-of-swapped-faces)

We worked around the limitation of the models by introducing the pixel boost option.

#### What operating systems are supported?[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/faq#what-operating-systems-are-supported)

We support latest versions of Linux, macOS and Windows.

#### How much VRAM should my GPU have?[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/faq#how-much-vram-should-my-gpu-have)

We consider 8GB as the bare minimum, everything from 12GB should work fine.

#### Where can temporary resources be found?[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/faq#where-can-temporary-resources-be-found)

Temporary resources are stored inside `.facefusion` located in the temporary directory of your operating system.

#### What causes the program to halt after 100% analysing?[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/faq#what-causes-the-program-to-halt-after-100-analysing)

We don't allow NSFW content due to ethical considerations.

# Windows | 3.7.1 | FaceFusion

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.facefusion.io/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/troubleshooting/windows.md)
.

#### Upgrading to Windows 11 caused a performance hit[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/troubleshooting/windows#upgrading-to-windows-11-caused-a-performance-hit)

Turn off real-time search in Windows Defender or exclude temporary resources.

#### DLL load failed while importing cv2[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/troubleshooting/windows#dll-load-failed-while-importing-cv2)

The absence of the Media Feature Pack can lead to issues with OpenCV.

# FAQ | FaceFusion

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.facefusion.io/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.facefusion.io/faq.md)
.

#### Are there plans to implement a high resolution inswapper?[](https://docs.facefusion.io/faq#are-there-plans-to-implement-a-high-resolution-inswapper)

InsightFace opted against releasing those models due ethical considerations.

#### How to improve the quality of swapped faces?[](https://docs.facefusion.io/faq#how-to-improve-the-quality-of-swapped-faces)

We worked around the limitation of the models by introducing the pixel boost option.

#### What operating systems are supported?[](https://docs.facefusion.io/faq#what-operating-systems-are-supported)

We support latest versions of Linux, macOS and Windows.

#### How much VRAM should my GPU have?[](https://docs.facefusion.io/faq#how-much-vram-should-my-gpu-have)

We consider 8GB as the bare minimum, everything from 12GB should work fine.

#### Where can temporary resources be found?[](https://docs.facefusion.io/faq#where-can-temporary-resources-be-found)

Temporary resources are stored inside `.facefusion` located in the temporary directory of your operating system.

#### What causes the program to halt after 100% analysing?[](https://docs.facefusion.io/faq#what-causes-the-program-to-halt-after-100-analysing)

We don't allow NSFW content due to ethical considerations.

# Disclaimer | FaceFusion

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.facefusion.io/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.facefusion.io/introduction/disclaimer.md)
.

We acknowledge the unethical potential of FaceFusion and are resolutely dedicated to establishing safeguards against such misuse. This software has been engineered to abstain from processing inappropriate content such as nudity, graphic content and sensitive material.

It is important to note that we maintain a strong stance against any type of pornographic nature and do not collaborate with any websites promoting the unauthorized use of our software.

Users who seek to engage in such activities will face consequences, including being banned from our community or reported to public authorities in case of legal violations.

---

# Troubleshooting | 3.7.1 | FaceFusion

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.facefusion.io/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/troubleshooting.md)
.

#### Bad dependencies resolutions[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/troubleshooting#bad-dependencies-resolutions)

Make sure to run Python 3.12 and latest version of PIP to prevent dependencies conflicts.

#### No matching distribution found for 'xxx'[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/troubleshooting#no-matching-distribution-found-for-xxx)

Make sure to run the latest version of your operating system.

#### No module named 'xxx'[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/troubleshooting#no-module-named-xxx)

A `ModuleNotFoundError` indicates that dependencies have not been correctly installed.

#### Module 'xxx' has no attribute 'xxx'[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/troubleshooting#module-xxx-has-no-attribute-xxx)

A `AttributeError` indicates that dependencies have not been correctly installed.

#### Protobuf parsing failed[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/troubleshooting#protobuf-parsing-failed)

Delete the broken model from the `.assets/models` directory to enforce its re-download.

#### Cannot allocate memory[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/troubleshooting#cannot-allocate-memory)

Memory exceptions during processing are usually cause when the GPU runs out of VRAM.

# Face Vocabulary | 3.7.1 | FaceFusion

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.facefusion.io/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/knowledgebase/face-vocabulary.md)
.

### Face Analyser[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/knowledgebase/face-vocabulary#face-analyser)

> Describes the part of the software that is responsible to detect and identify faces.

### Face Reference[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/knowledgebase/face-vocabulary#face-reference)

> Describes the snapshot of a face being used for the face matching during processing frames.

### Face Matching[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/knowledgebase/face-vocabulary#face-matching)

> Describes the process of comparing two faces against a given threshold, called face distance.

### Face Distance[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/knowledgebase/face-vocabulary#face-distance)

> Describes how similar two faces are.

### Face Score[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/knowledgebase/face-vocabulary#face-score)

> Describes the confidence of the face detection.

### Face Bouncing[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/knowledgebase/face-vocabulary#face-bouncing)

> Describes the flicker between persons during processing frames as of failing face recognition.

### Face Jittering[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/knowledgebase/face-vocabulary#face-jittering)

> Describes the moving facial features during processing frames as of unstable facial landmarks.

*   [Face Analyser](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/knowledgebase/face-vocabulary#face-analyser)
    
*   [Face Reference](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/knowledgebase/face-vocabulary#face-reference)
    
*   [Face Matching](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/knowledgebase/face-vocabulary#face-matching)

# UI Layout | FaceFusion

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.facefusion.io/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.facefusion.io/workshop/ui-layout.md)
.

### 1\. Create[](https://docs.facefusion.io/workshop/ui-layout#id-1.-create)

Create a new example file:

Copy

    facefusion/uis/layouts/example.py

### 2\. Implement[](https://docs.facefusion.io/workshop/ui-layout#id-2.-implement)

Implement the hooks of the UI layout:

Copy

    import gradio
    
    from facefusion.uis.components import example
    
    
    def pre_check() -> bool:
    	return True
    
    
    def render() -> gradio.Blocks:
    	with gradio.Blocks() as layout:
    		example.render()
    	return layout
    
    
    def listen() -> None:
    	example.listen()
    
    
    def run(ui : gradio.Blocks) -> None:
    	ui.launch()

### 3\. Done[](https://docs.facefusion.io/workshop/ui-layout#id-3.-done)

Finally, run the program:

*   [1\. Create](https://docs.facefusion.io/workshop/ui-layout#id-1.-create)
    
*   [2\. Implement](https://docs.facefusion.io/workshop/ui-layout#id-2.-implement)
    
*   [3\. Done](https://docs.facefusion.io/workshop/ui-layout#id-3.-done)
    

Copy

    python run.py --ui-layouts example

# UI Component | FaceFusion

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.facefusion.io/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.facefusion.io/workshop/ui-component.md)
.

### 1\. Create[](https://docs.facefusion.io/workshop/ui-component#id-1.-create)

Create a new example file:

Copy

    facefusion/uis/components/example.py

### 2\. Implement[](https://docs.facefusion.io/workshop/ui-component#id-2.-implement)

Implement the basics of the UI component:

Copy

    from typing import Optional
    import gradio
    
    from facefusion.uis.typing import Update
    
    EXAMPLE_IMAGE : Optional[gradio.Image] = None
    
    
    def render() -> None:
    	global EXAMPLE_IMAGE
    
    	EXAMPLE_IMAGE = gradio.Image()
    
    
    def listen() -> None:
    	EXAMPLE_IMAGE.change(update, inputs = EXAMPLE_IMAGE, outputs = EXAMPLE_IMAGE)
    
    
    def update() -> Update:
    	return gradio.update()

### 3\. Done[](https://docs.facefusion.io/workshop/ui-component#id-3.-done)

Finally, add the component:

*   [1\. Create](https://docs.facefusion.io/workshop/ui-component#id-1.-create)
    
*   [2\. Implement](https://docs.facefusion.io/workshop/ui-component#id-2.-implement)
    
*   [3\. Done](https://docs.facefusion.io/workshop/ui-component#id-3.-done)
    

Copy

    from facefusion.uis.components import example

# Deepfake Webcam | FaceFusion

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.facefusion.io/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.facefusion.io/usage/deepfake-webcam.md)
.

Copy

    python facefusion.py run --ui-layouts webcam

### Modes[](https://docs.facefusion.io/usage/deepfake-webcam#modes)

#### Inline[](https://docs.facefusion.io/usage/deepfake-webcam#inline)

Render the webcam feed within the UI.

#### UDP[](https://docs.facefusion.io/usage/deepfake-webcam#udp)

A stream at `udp://localhost:27000` can be captured by OBS.

#### V4L2[](https://docs.facefusion.io/usage/deepfake-webcam#v4l2)

A stream at `/dev/video*` can be captured by OBS.

*   [Usage](https://docs.facefusion.io/usage/deepfake-webcam#usage)
    
*   [Modes](https://docs.facefusion.io/usage/deepfake-webcam#modes)

Copy

    facefusion/processors/modules/example.py

### 2\. Implement[](https://docs.facefusion.io/workshop/processor#id-2.-implement)

Define the inputs:

Copy

    ExampleInputs = TypedDict('ExampleInputs',
    {
    	'reference_vision_frame' : VisionFrame,
    	'source_faces' : List[Face],
    	'target_vision_frame' : VisionFrame
    })

Implement the hooks of the processor:

Copy

    from argparse import ArgumentParser
    
    from facefusion.processors.typing import ExampleInputs
    
    from facefusion.typing import ApplyStateItem, Args, Face, InferencePool, ProcessMode, VisionFrame
    
    
    def get_inference_pool() -> InferencePool:
    	pass
    
    
    def clear_inference_pool() -> None:
    	pass
    
    
    def register_args(program : ArgumentParser) -> None:
    	pass
    
    
    def apply_args(args : Args, apply_state_item : ApplyStateItem) -> None:
    	pass
    
    
    def pre_check() -> bool:
    	return True
    
    
    def pre_process(mode : ProcessMode) -> bool:
    	pass
    
    
    def post_process() -> None:
    	pass
    
    
    def process_frame(inputs : ExampleInputs) -> VisionFrame:
    	pass
    

### 3\. Done[](https://docs.facefusion.io/workshop/processor#id-3.-done)

Finally, run the program:

*   [1\. Create](https://docs.facefusion.io/workshop/processor#id-1.-create)
    
*   [2\. Implement](https://docs.facefusion.io/workshop/processor#id-2.-implement)
    
*   [3\. Done](https://docs.facefusion.io/workshop/processor#id-3.-done)

# Run With Docker | FaceFusion

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.facefusion.io/llms.txt)

.

### Installation[](https://docs.facefusion.io/usage/run-with-docker#installation)

Clone the repository:

Copy

    git clone https://github.com/facefusion/facefusion-docker.git

Run the `CPU` container:

Copy

    docker compose -f docker-compose.cpu.yml up

Run the `CUDA` container:

Copy

    docker compose -f docker-compose.cuda.yml up

Run the `TensorRT` container:

Copy

    docker compose -f docker-compose.tensorrt.yml up

Run the `ROCm` container:

Copy

    docker compose -f docker-compose.rocm.yml up

### Usage[](https://docs.facefusion.io/usage/run-with-docker#usage)

Browse the `CPU` container:

Copy

    http://localhost:7865

Browse the `CUDA` container:

Copy

    http://localhost:7870

Browse the `TensorRT` container:

Browse the `ROCm` container:

*   [Installation](https://docs.facefusion.io/usage/run-with-docker#installation)
    
*   [Usage](https://docs.facefusion.io/usage/run-with-docker#usage)
    

Copy

    http://localhost:7875

Copy

.

### Installation[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/run-with-docker#installation)

Clone the repository:

Copy

    git clone https://github.com/facefusion/facefusion-docker.git

Run the `CPU` container:

Copy

    docker compose -f docker-compose.cpu.yml up

Run the `CUDA` container:

Copy

    docker compose -f docker-compose.cuda.yml up

Run the `TensorRT` container:

Copy

    docker compose -f docker-compose.tensorrt.yml up

Run the `ROCm` container:

Copy

    docker compose -f docker-compose.rocm.yml up

### Usage[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/run-with-docker#usage)

Browse the `CPU` container:

Copy

    http://localhost:7865

Browse the `CUDA` container:

Copy

    http://localhost:7870

Browse the `TensorRT` container:

Browse the `ROCm` container:

*   [Installation](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/run-with-docker#installation)
    
*   [Usage](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/run-with-docker#usage)
    

Copy

    http://localhost:7875

Copy

    http://localhost:7880

# Processor | 3.7.1 | FaceFusion

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.facefusion.io/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/workshop/processor.md)
.

### 1\. Create[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/workshop/processor#id-1.-create)

Create a new example file:

Copy

    facefusion/processors/modules/example.py

### 2\. Implement[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/workshop/processor#id-2.-implement)

Define the inputs:

Copy

    ExampleInputs = TypedDict('ExampleInputs',
    {
    	'reference_vision_frame' : VisionFrame,
    	'source_faces' : List[Face],
    	'target_vision_frame' : VisionFrame
    })

Implement the hooks of the processor:

Copy

    from argparse import ArgumentParser
    
    from facefusion.processors.typing import ExampleInputs
    
    from facefusion.typing import ApplyStateItem, Args, Face, InferencePool, ProcessMode, VisionFrame
    
    
    def get_inference_pool() -> InferencePool:
    	pass
    
    
    def clear_inference_pool() -> None:
    	pass
    
    
    def register_args(program : ArgumentParser) -> None:
    	pass
    
    
    def apply_args(args : Args, apply_state_item : ApplyStateItem) -> None:
    	pass
    
    
    def pre_check() -> bool:
    	return True
    
    
    def pre_process(mode : ProcessMode) -> bool:
    	pass
    
    
    def post_process() -> None:
    	pass
    
    
    def process_frame(inputs : ExampleInputs) -> VisionFrame:
    	pass
    

### 3\. Done[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/workshop/processor#id-3.-done)

Finally, run the program:

*   [1\. Create](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/workshop/processor#id-1.-create)
    
*   [2\. Implement](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/workshop/processor#id-2.-implement)
    
*   [3\. Done](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/workshop/processor#id-3.-done)
    

Copy

    python run.py --processors example

[Windows](https://docs.facefusion.io/installation/platform/windows)

### 2\. Prepare Your Environment[](https://docs.facefusion.io/installation#id-2.-prepare-your-environment)

Initialize conda for your terminal:

Copy

    conda init --all

Create the environment:

Copy

    conda create --name facefusion python=3.12 pip=25.0

Activate the environment:

Copy

    conda activate facefusion

# Job Runner | 3.7.1 | FaceFusion

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.facefusion.io/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-commands/job-runner.md)
.

### Job Run[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-commands/job-runner#job-run)

Run a queued job.

Command: `job-run [job_id]`

Arguments:

*   Paths
    
    *   [Config Path](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-arguments/paths#config-path)
        
    *   [Temp Path](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-arguments/paths#temp-path)
        
    *   [Jobs Path](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-arguments/paths#jobs-path)
        
    
*   Download
    
    *   [Download Providers](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-arguments/download#download-providers)
        
    
*   [Execution](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-arguments/execution)
    
*   [Memory](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-arguments/memory)
    
*   Misc
    
    *   [Log Level](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-arguments/misc#log-level)
        
    

Example: `job-run example`

### Job Run All[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-commands/job-runner#job-run-all)

Run all queued jobs.

Command: `job-run-all`

Arguments:

*   Paths
    
    *   [Config Path](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-arguments/paths#config-path)
        
    *   [Temp Path](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-arguments/paths#temp-path)
        
    *   [Jobs Path](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-arguments/paths#jobs-path)
        
    
*   Download
    
    *   [Download Providers](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-arguments/download#download-providers)
        
    
*   [Execution](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-arguments/execution)
    
*   [Memory](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-arguments/memory)
    
*   [Misc](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-arguments/misc)
    

Example: `job-run-all`

### Job Retry[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-commands/job-runner#job-retry)

Retry a failed job.

Command: `job-retry [job_id]`

Arguments:

*   Paths
    
    *   [Config Path](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-arguments/paths#config-path)
        
    *   [Temp Path](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-arguments/paths#temp-path)

*   Download
    
    *   [Download Providers](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-arguments/download#download-providers)
        
    
*   [Execution](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-arguments/execution)
    
*   [Memory](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-arguments/memory)
    
*   Misc
    
    *   [Log Level](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-arguments/misc#log-level)
        
    

Example: `job-retry example`

### Job Retry All[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-commands/job-runner#job-retry-all)

Retry all failed jobs.

Command: `job-retry-all`

Arguments:

*   Paths
    
    *   [Config Path](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-arguments/paths#config-path)

*   [Execution](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-arguments/execution)
    
*   [Memory](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-arguments/memory)
    
*   [Misc](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-arguments/misc)
    

Example: `job-retry-all`

# Job Manager | 3.7.1 | FaceFusion

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://docs.facefusion.io/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-commands/job-manager.md)
.

### Job List[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-commands/job-manager#job-list)

List jobs by status.

Command: `job-list [job_status]`

Arguments:

*   Paths
    
    *   [Jobs Path](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-arguments/paths#jobs-path)
        
    
*   Misc
    
    *   [Log Level](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-arguments/misc#log-level)
        
    

Example: `job-list drafted`

### Job Create[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-commands/job-manager#job-create)

Create a drafted job.

Command: `job-create [job_id]`

Arguments:

*   Paths
    
    *   [Jobs Path](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-arguments/paths#jobs-path)
        
    
*   Misc
    
    *   [Log Level](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-arguments/misc#log-level)
        
    

Example: `job-create example`

### Job Submit[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-commands/job-manager#job-submit)

Submit a drafted job to become a queued job.

Command: `job-submit [job_id]`

Arguments:

*   Paths
    
    *   [Jobs Path](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-arguments/paths#jobs-path)
        
    
*   Misc
    
    *   [Log Level](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-arguments/misc#log-level)
        
    

Example: `job-submit example`

### Job Submit All[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-commands/job-manager#job-submit-all)

Submit all drafted jobs to become a queued jobs.

Command: `job-submit-all`

Arguments:

*   Paths
    
    *   [Jobs Path](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-arguments/paths#jobs-path)
        
    
*   [Misc](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-arguments/misc)
    

Example: `job-submit-all`

### Job Delete[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-commands/job-manager#job-delete)

Delete a drafted, queued, failed or completed job.

Command: `job-delete [job_id]`

Arguments:

*   Paths
    
    *   [Jobs Path](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-arguments/paths#jobs-path)
        
    
*   Misc
    
    *   [Log Level](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-arguments/misc#log-level)
        
    

Example: `job-delete example`

### Job Delete All[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-commands/job-manager#job-delete-all)

Delete all drafted, queued, failed and completed jobs.

Command: `job-delete-all`

Arguments:

*   Paths
    
    *   [Jobs Path](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-arguments/paths#jobs-path)
        
    
*   [Misc](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-arguments/misc)
    

Example: `job-delete-all`

### Job Add Step[](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-commands/job-manager#job-add-step)

Add a step to a drafted job.

Command: `job-add-step [job_id]`

Arguments:

*   Paths
    
    *   [Config Path](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-arguments/paths#config-path)
        
    *   [Jobs Path](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-arguments/paths#jobs-path)

    *   [Target Path](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-arguments/paths#target-path)
        
    *   [Output Path](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-arguments/paths#output-path)
        
    
*   [Face Detector](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-arguments/face-detector)
    
*   [Face Landmarker](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-arguments/face-landmarker)
    
*   [Face Selector](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-arguments/face-selector)
    
*   [Face Masker](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-arguments/face-masker)
    
*   [Frame Extraction](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-arguments/frame-extraction)
    
*   [Output Creation](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-arguments/output-creation)
    
*   [Processors](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-arguments/processors)
    
*   Misc
    
    *   [Log Level](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-arguments/misc#log-level)
        
    

Example: `job-add-step example`

*   [Output Creation](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-arguments/output-creation)
    
*   [Processors](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-arguments/processors)
    
*   Misc
    
    *   [Log Level](https://docs.facefusion.io/3.7.1/usage/cli-arguments/misc#log-level)
